Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the nonlinear framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 363. The resulting operator achieves bound O(382/n^1).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_363(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 363. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^382(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.6 (Bound Derivation)

Theorem 214.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: For any nonlinear module M satisfying the homomorphism theorem hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 753. The projective resolution has length at most 101 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.
